- path: new_merging_feature _delete_later\orders_screen.dart
  content: |
      import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
      import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
      import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
      
      import '../../../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
      import '../../../../core/utils/currency.dart';
      import '../../../auth/presentation/providers/auth_provider.dart';
      import '../../../search/presentation/providers/medicine_provider.dart';
      import '../../data/models/order_history_model.dart';
      import '../../data/repositories/order_history_repository.dart';
      import '../providers/orders_provider.dart';
      
      /// The Orders tab: new orders waiting for a decision.
      ///
      /// Only [OrderStatus.new_] orders appear here. Approving or rejecting one
      /// moves it out of this list, because the decision is written to the same
      /// stream this screen reads. The history icon opens what has already been
      /// decided.
      class OrdersScreen extends StatefulWidget {
        const OrdersScreen({super.key});
      
        @override
        State<OrdersScreen> createState() => _OrdersScreenState();
      }
      
      class _OrdersScreenState extends State<OrdersScreen> {
        /// Created once per visit. Building the stream inside [build] would open a
        /// new listener on every rebuild and reset the list to a spinner each time.
        late final Stream<List<OrderHistory>> _orders;
      
        @override
        void initState() {
          super.initState();
          final uid = context.read<AuthProvider>().user?.id ?? '';
          _orders = context.read<OrderHistoryRepositoryFactory>()(uid).watchAll();
        }
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          return Scaffold(
            appBar: AppBar(
              title: const Text('Orders'),
              centerTitle: true,
              actions: [
                IconButton(
                  tooltip: 'Order history',
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.history),
                  onPressed: () => context.pushNamed('orders-history'),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            body: StreamBuilder<List<OrderHistory>>(
              stream: _orders,
              builder: (context, snapshot) {
                if (snapshot.hasError) {
                  // The raw error stays out of the UI: Firestore messages are for
                  // developers. Log it so it can still be traced.
                  debugPrint('Orders stream failed: ${snapshot.error}');
                  return const _LoadError();
                }
      
                if (!snapshot.hasData) {
                  return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
                }
      
                final pending =
                    snapshot.data!.where((o) => o.status == OrderStatus.new_).toList()
                      ..sort((a, b) => b.time.compareTo(a.time));
      
                if (pending.isEmpty) {
                  return const _EmptyOrderList();
                }
      
                return ListView.separated(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  itemCount: pending.length,
                  separatorBuilder: (_, _) => const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) =>
                      _PendingOrderCard(order: pending[index]),
                );
              },
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      
      class _LoadError extends StatelessWidget {
        const _LoadError();
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          final theme = Theme.of(context);
      
          return Center(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.error_outline, size: 48, color: theme.colorScheme.error),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text(
                    'Orders could not be loaded',
                    style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text(
                    'Check the connection and try again later.',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                      color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      
      class _EmptyOrderList extends StatelessWidget {
        const _EmptyOrderList();
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          final theme = Theme.of(context);
      
          return Center(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Icon(
                  Icons.inbox_outlined,
                  size: 64,
                  color: theme.colorScheme.outline,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                Text(
                  'No orders waiting',
                  style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  'New orders waiting for approval will appear here.',
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.outline,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      
      /// One pending order, with the two buttons that move it out of the list.
      class _PendingOrderCard extends StatefulWidget {
        const _PendingOrderCard({required this.order});
      
        final OrderHistory order;
      
        @override
        State<_PendingOrderCard> createState() => _PendingOrderCardState();
      }
      
      class _PendingOrderCardState extends State<_PendingOrderCard> {
        /// True while a decision is being written.
        ///
        /// The card leaves the list once the status lands, but the stream takes a
        /// round trip to say so. Without this a second tap in that gap would approve
        /// the same order twice, and take its lines off the shelf twice with it.
        bool _busy = false;
      
        OrderHistory get order => widget.order;
      
        /// Units across every line, which is what the counter means by "items".
        int get _units => order.items.fold(0, (sum, line) => sum + line.quantity);
      
        Future<void> _approve() async {
          if (_busy) return;
          setState(() => _busy = true);
      
          // Read before the first await: after it the card may have been disposed.
          final orders = context.read<OrdersProvider>();
          final catalogue = context.read<MedicineProvider>();
      
          final updated = await orders.updateOrderStatus(
            order.id,
            OrderStatus.approved,
          );
          if (!updated) {
            if (mounted) {
              setState(() => _busy = false);
              _showUpdateFailure();
            }
            return;
          }
      
          // The shelf comes down only once the order is approved. A pending or
          // rejected order never left the counter, so its lines are left alone.
          await catalogue.recordSale([
            for (final line in order.items)
              (medicine: line.medicine, quantity: line.quantity),
          ]);
      
          if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
        }
      
        Future<void> _reject() async {
          if (_busy) return;
          setState(() => _busy = true);
      
          final orders = context.read<OrdersProvider>();
      
          final reason = await showDialog<String>(
            context: context,
            builder: (_) => _RejectOrderDialog(orderId: order.id),
          );
          if (reason == null) {
            if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
            return;
          }
      
          final updated = await orders.updateOrderStatus(
            order.id,
            OrderStatus.rejected,
            rejectionReason: reason,
          );
          if (!mounted) return;
          setState(() => _busy = false);
          if (!updated) _showUpdateFailure();
        }
      
        void _showUpdateFailure() {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            const SnackBar(content: Text('Could not update this order. Try again.')),
          );
        }
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          final theme = Theme.of(context);
      
          return Card(
            elevation: 0,
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
              side: BorderSide(color: theme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: ExpansionTile(
              key: PageStorageKey(order.id),
              tilePadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
              leading: CircleAvatar(
                backgroundColor: order.statusBackgroundColor,
                child: Icon(Icons.pending_actions, color: order.statusColor),
              ),
              title: Text(
                'Order #${order.id}',
                style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
              subtitle: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
                child: Text(
                  '$_units ${_units == 1 ? 'item' : 'items'} · '
                  '${order.formattedDate(context: context)}',
                  style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              trailing: Text(
                formatCentavos(order.totalCentavos),
                style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: theme.colorScheme.primary,
                ),
              ),
              children: [
                const Divider(height: 1),
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        'Order lines',
                        style: theme.textTheme.labelLarge?.copyWith(
                          color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      for (final line in order.items)
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3),
                          child: Row(
                            children: [
                              Expanded(
                                child: Text(
                                  '${line.quantity}x ${line.medicine.name}',
                                  style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium,
                                ),
                              ),
                              Text(
                                formatCentavos(line.totalCentavos),
                                style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                              onPressed: _busy ? null : _reject,
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.close, size: 18),
                              label: const Text('Reject'),
                              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                                foregroundColor: theme.colorScheme.error,
                                side: BorderSide(color: theme.colorScheme.error),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 12),
                          Expanded(
                            child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                              onPressed: _busy ? null : _approve,
                              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                                backgroundColor: AppColors.brandGreenSurface,
                                foregroundColor: AppColors.brandGreen,
                                side: const BorderSide(
                                  color: AppColors.brandGreenSurfaceBorder,
                                ),
                              ),
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.check, size: 18),
                              label: const Text('Approve'),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      
      class _RejectOrderDialog extends StatefulWidget {
        const _RejectOrderDialog({required this.orderId});
      
        final String orderId;
      
        @override
        State<_RejectOrderDialog> createState() => _RejectOrderDialogState();
      }
      
      class _RejectOrderDialogState extends State<_RejectOrderDialog> {
        final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
        final _reasonController = TextEditingController();
        String? _selectedReason;
        bool _showSelectionError = false;
      
        static const _presetReasons = [
          (label: 'Item is out of stock', icon: Icons.inventory_2_outlined),
          (
            label: 'Store is closed or not accepting orders',
            icon: Icons.storefront_outlined,
          ),
          (
            label: 'Address is outside our service area',
            icon: Icons.location_off_outlined,
          ),
          (
            label: 'Payment could not be verified',
            icon: Icons.credit_card_off_outlined,
          ),
          (label: 'Duplicate order', icon: Icons.content_copy_outlined),
        ];
      
        static const _otherReason = 'Other (write your own reason)';
      
        @override
        void dispose() {
          _reasonController.dispose();
          super.dispose();
        }
      
        void _submit() {
          if (_selectedReason == null) {
            setState(() => _showSelectionError = true);
            return;
          }
          if (_selectedReason == _otherReason && !_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
            return;
          }
      
          Navigator.of(context).pop(
            _selectedReason == _otherReason
                ? _reasonController.text.trim()
                : _selectedReason,
          );
        }
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          return AlertDialog(
            title: const Text('Reject this order?'),
            scrollable: true,
            content: Form(
              key: _formKey,
              child: ConstrainedBox(
                constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 480),
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text('Select a reason for rejecting Order #${widget.orderId}.'),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    for (final reason in [
                      ..._presetReasons.map((option) => option.label),
                      _otherReason,
                    ])
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                        child: _ReasonOption(
                          label: reason,
                          icon: reason == _otherReason
                              ? Icons.edit_outlined
                              : _presetReasons
                                    .firstWhere((option) => option.label == reason)
                                    .icon,
                          selected: _selectedReason == reason,
                          onTap: () => setState(() {
                            _selectedReason = reason;
                            _showSelectionError = false;
                          }),
                        ),
                      ),
                    if (_showSelectionError) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                        'Please select a reason.',
                        style: TextStyle(color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error),
                      ),
                    ],
                    if (_selectedReason == _otherReason) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _reasonController,
                        autofocus: true,
                        maxLength: 500,
                        maxLines: 3,
                        textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.sentences,
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          labelText: 'Your reason',
                          hintText: 'Enter the reason for rejection',
                          border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                          alignLabelWithHint: true,
                        ),
                        validator: (value) => value == null || value.trim().isEmpty
                            ? 'Please enter a reason.'
                            : null,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            actions: [
              TextButton(
                onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                child: const Text('Cancel'),
              ),
              OutlinedButton(
                onPressed: _submit,
                style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: AppColors.brandGreenSurface,
                  foregroundColor: AppColors.brandGreen,
                  side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.brandGreenSurfaceBorder),
                ),
                child: const Text('Reject'),
              ),
            ],
          );
        }
      }
      
      class _ReasonOption extends StatelessWidget {
        const _ReasonOption({
          required this.label,
          required this.icon,
          required this.selected,
          required this.onTap,
        });
      
        final String label;
        final IconData icon;
        final bool selected;
        final VoidCallback onTap;
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          final theme = Theme.of(context);
          final scheme = theme.colorScheme;
      
          return Material(
            color: selected ? scheme.primaryContainer : Colors.transparent,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            child: InkWell(
              onTap: onTap,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              child: Container(
                constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 48),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  border: Border.all(
                    color: selected ? scheme.primary : scheme.outlineVariant,
                  ),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      icon,
                      color: selected ? scheme.primary : scheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Expanded(child: Text(label)),
                    if (selected) Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: scheme.primary),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      
- path: new_merging_feature _delete_later\order_history_screen.dart
  content: |
      import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
      import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
      
      import '../../../../core/utils/currency.dart';
      import '../../../../core/widgets/pagination_bar.dart';
      import '../../../auth/presentation/providers/auth_provider.dart';
      import '../../data/models/order_history_model.dart';
      import '../../data/repositories/order_history_repository.dart';
      import '../providers/order_history_provider.dart';
      
      /// The Orders tab's history: approved and rejected orders, searchable.
      ///
      /// Split out from the Order List because the two answer different questions.
      /// The list is work to do -- new orders waiting for a decision. History is a
      /// record -- what was decided and when. A single screen trying to be both puts
      /// the thing that needs action next to a hundred things that do not.
      ///
      /// Reached from the Order List's AppBar. Nested under it so the back arrow
      /// returns to the list rather than to another tab.
      ///
      /// A [StatelessWidget] whose only job is to put the provider above the body.
      /// The body's own `context` is what every `context.read` inside it resolves
      /// against, and Provider only looks *up* the tree -- a provider created inside
      /// the same `build()` that returns it would be invisible to that widget's own
      /// methods, which is exactly why the screen was silent before.
      class OrderHistoryScreen extends StatelessWidget {
        const OrderHistoryScreen({super.key});
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          return ChangeNotifierProvider<OrderHistoryProvider>(
            create: (ctx) {
              final uid = ctx.read<AuthProvider>().user?.id ?? '';
              final repository = ctx.read<OrderHistoryRepositoryFactory>()(uid);
              return OrderHistoryProvider(repository: repository);
            },
            child: const _OrderHistoryBody(),
          );
        }
      }
      
      /// Everything below the provider. Its `context` can see it.
      class _OrderHistoryBody extends StatefulWidget {
        const _OrderHistoryBody();
      
        @override
        State<_OrderHistoryBody> createState() => _OrderHistoryBodyState();
      }
      
      class _OrderHistoryBodyState extends State<_OrderHistoryBody> {
        final TextEditingController _controller = TextEditingController();
      
        static const int _pageSize = 10;
      
        /// Which page of the filtered list is on screen. Clamped on read rather
        /// than written -- see [_effectivePage].
        int _currentPage = 1;
      
        @override
        void dispose() {
          _controller.dispose();
          super.dispose();
        }
      
        /// The page number actually rendered, which is [_currentPage] pulled back
        /// inside the current range of pages.
        ///
        /// An approval made on the Order List, or a filter change, can drop the
        /// visible list below the page the user was on. Corrected on read rather
        /// than reset on every change, so a user on a valid page stays there.
        int _effectivePage(int pageCount) => _currentPage.clamp(1, pageCount);
      
        /// Pages the current filter and query produce, at least one so the range
        /// arithmetic has something to hold onto even for an empty list.
        static int _pageCountFor(int itemCount) =>
            itemCount == 0 ? 1 : (itemCount + _pageSize - 1) ~/ _pageSize;
      
        void _resetPage() {
          if (_currentPage != 1) setState(() => _currentPage = 1);
        }
      
        void _onQueryChanged(String value) {
          context.read<OrderHistoryProvider>().updateQuery(value);
          _resetPage();
        }
      
        void _clearSearch() {
          _controller.clear();
          context.read<OrderHistoryProvider>().clearQuery();
          _resetPage();
        }
      
        void _onFilterSelected(HistoryFilter filter) {
          context.read<OrderHistoryProvider>().setFilter(filter);
          _resetPage();
        }
      
        Future<void> _jumpToPage(int pageCount, int current) async {
          final entered = await showDialog<int>(
            context: context,
            builder: (_) => JumpToPageDialog(
              current: current,
              maxPage: pageCount,
            ),
          );
          if (entered == null || !mounted) return;
          setState(() => _currentPage = entered);
        }
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          final theme = Theme.of(context);
          final provider = context.watch<OrderHistoryProvider>();
          final results = provider.results;
      
          final pageCount = _pageCountFor(results.length);
          final page = _effectivePage(pageCount);
          final startIndex = (page - 1) * _pageSize;
          final pageItems = results
              .skip(startIndex)
              .take(_pageSize)
              .toList(growable: false);
          final firstItem = results.isEmpty ? 0 : startIndex + 1;
          final lastItem = results.isEmpty
              ? 0
              : (startIndex + pageItems.length).clamp(0, results.length);
      
          return Scaffold(
            appBar: AppBar(
              title: const Text('Order History'),
              centerTitle: true,
            ),
            body: Column(
              children: [
                _SearchRow(
                  controller: _controller,
                  onChanged: _onQueryChanged,
                  onClear: _clearSearch,
                ),
                _FilterBar(
                  selected: provider.filter,
                  counts: provider.counts,
                  onSelected: _onFilterSelected,
                ),
                Expanded(
                  child: switch ((provider.error, provider.isLoading)) {
                    (final String message, _) => _Message(
                        icon: Icons.cloud_off_outlined,
                        title: 'History did not load',
                        detail: message,
                        colour: theme.colorScheme.error,
                      ),
                    (_, true) => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                    _ when provider.totalCount == 0 => const _Message(
                        icon: Icons.receipt_long_outlined,
                        title: 'No orders in history yet',
                        detail: 'Approved and rejected orders appear here once '
                            'a cashier acts on them.',
                      ),
                    _ when results.isEmpty => _Message(
                        icon: Icons.search_off_outlined,
                        title: 'Nothing matches',
                        detail: provider.query.isEmpty
                            ? 'No order has this status.'
                            : 'No order matches "${provider.query.trim()}".',
                      ),
                    _ => ListView.separated(
                        // Keyed on the page so the list scroll position resets when
                        // the page changes.
                        key: ValueKey('history-page-$page'),
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
                        itemCount: pageItems.length,
                        separatorBuilder: (_, _) => const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        itemBuilder: (context, index) => Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                          child: _HistoryCard(order: pageItems[index]),
                        ),
                      ),
                  },
                ),
                // Drawn only when there is more than one page. An empty list, an
                // error, or a page worth of results all render the body alone -- a
                // pager reading "Page 1 of 1" is chrome with nothing to say.
                if (pageCount > 1 &&
                    provider.error == null &&
                    !provider.isLoading &&
                    results.isNotEmpty)
                  PaginationBar(
                    page: page,
                    pageCount: pageCount,
                    firstItem: firstItem,
                    lastItem: lastItem,
                    totalItems: results.length,
                    onPrevious: page > 1
                        ? () => setState(() => _currentPage = page - 1)
                        : null,
                    onNext: page < pageCount
                        ? () => setState(() => _currentPage = page + 1)
                        : null,
                    onTapPage: () => _jumpToPage(pageCount, page),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Search and filter
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
      
      class _SearchRow extends StatelessWidget {
        const _SearchRow({
          required this.controller,
          required this.onChanged,
          required this.onClear,
        });
      
        final TextEditingController controller;
        final ValueChanged<String> onChanged;
        final VoidCallback onClear;
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          return Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 8),
            child: TextField(
              controller: controller,
              onChanged: onChanged,
              textInputAction: TextInputAction.search,
              autocorrect: false,
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                hintText: 'Search orders',
                prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search),
                suffixIcon: controller.text.isEmpty
                    ? null
                    : IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.close),
                        tooltip: 'Clear',
                        onPressed: onClear,
                      ),
                filled: true,
                border: OutlineInputBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(28),
                  borderSide: BorderSide.none,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      
      class _FilterBar extends StatelessWidget {
        const _FilterBar({
          required this.selected,
          required this.counts,
          required this.onSelected,
        });
      
        final HistoryFilter selected;
        final Map<HistoryFilter, int> counts;
        final ValueChanged<HistoryFilter> onSelected;
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          return SizedBox(
            height: 52,
            child: ListView(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
              children: [
                for (final filter in HistoryFilter.values)
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8),
                    child: ChoiceChip(
                      showCheckmark: false,
                      selected: filter == selected,
                      onSelected: (_) => onSelected(filter),
                      label: Text('${filter.label} ${counts[filter] ?? 0}'),
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // History card
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
      
      /// One order in history. Expandable to show its lines. No action buttons:
      /// once an order is here, its decision has been made.
      class _HistoryCard extends StatelessWidget {
        const _HistoryCard({required this.order});
      
        final OrderHistory order;
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          final theme = Theme.of(context);
          final rejectionReason = order.rejectionReason;
      
          return Card(
            elevation: 0,
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
              side: BorderSide(color: theme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: ExpansionTile(
              key: PageStorageKey(order.id),
              tilePadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
              leading: CircleAvatar(
                backgroundColor: order.statusBackgroundColor,
                child: Icon(
                  _statusIcon(order.status),
                  color: order.statusColor,
                ),
              ),
              title: Text(
                'Order #${order.id}',
                style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
              subtitle: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4.0),
                child: Text(
                  '${order.items.length} item(s) · '
                  '${order.formattedDate(context: context)}',
                  style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              trailing: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    formatCentavos(order.totalCentavos),
                    style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: theme.colorScheme.primary,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  _StatusBadge(status: order.status),
                ],
              ),
              children: [
                const Divider(height: 1),
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        'Order Line Items',
                        style: theme.textTheme.labelLarge?.copyWith(
                          color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      for (final line in order.items) ...[
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3),
                          child: Row(
                            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                            children: [
                              Expanded(
                                child: Text(
                                  '${line.quantity}x ${line.medicine.name}',
                                  style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium,
                                ),
                              ),
                              Text(
                                formatCentavos(line.totalCentavos),
                                style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                      if (order.status == OrderStatus.rejected &&
                          rejectionReason != null &&
                          rejectionReason.trim().isNotEmpty) ...[
                        const SizedBox(height: 12),
                        Text(
                          'Rejection reason',
                          style: theme.textTheme.labelLarge?.copyWith(
                            color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                        Text(rejectionReason, style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        }
      
        static IconData _statusIcon(OrderStatus status) => switch (status) {
              OrderStatus.approved => Icons.check_circle_outline,
              OrderStatus.rejected => Icons.cancel_outlined,
              OrderStatus.new_ => Icons.pending_actions,
            };
      }
      
      class _StatusBadge extends StatelessWidget {
        const _StatusBadge({required this.status});
      
        final OrderStatus status;
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          final theme = Theme.of(context);
          final (backgroundColor, textColor, label) = switch (status) {
            OrderStatus.approved => (
                Colors.green.shade100,
                Colors.green.shade900,
                'APPROVED',
              ),
            OrderStatus.rejected => (
                theme.colorScheme.errorContainer,
                theme.colorScheme.onErrorContainer,
                'REJECTED',
              ),
            OrderStatus.new_ => (
                theme.colorScheme.primaryContainer,
                theme.colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer,
                'NEW',
              ),
          };
      
          return Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 2),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: backgroundColor,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
            ),
            child: Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 10,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: textColor,
              ),
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Empty / error bodies
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
      
      class _Message extends StatelessWidget {
        const _Message({
          required this.icon,
          required this.title,
          required this.detail,
          this.colour,
        });
      
        final IconData icon;
        final String title;
        final String detail;
        final Color? colour;
      
        @override
        Widget build(BuildContext context) {
          final theme = Theme.of(context);
      
          return Center(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Icon(icon, size: 48, color: colour ?? theme.colorScheme.outline),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text(
                    title,
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text(
                    detail,
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                      color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          );
        }
      }

- path: pubspec.yaml
  content: |
      name: pharmaguide
      description: "PharmaGuide - a pharmacy companion app with a medicine directory and first-aid guidance."
      # The following line prevents the package from being accidentally published to
      # pub.dev using `flutter pub publish`. This is preferred for private packages.
      publish_to: 'none' # Remove this line if you wish to publish to pub.dev
      
      # The following defines the version and build number for your application.
      # A version number is three numbers separated by dots, like 1.2.43
      # followed by an optional build number separated by a +.
      # Both the version and the builder number may be overridden in flutter
      # build by specifying --build-name and --build-number, respectively.
      # In Android, build-name is used as versionName while build-number used as versionCode.
      # Read more about Android versioning at https://developer.android.com/studio/publish/versioning
      # In iOS, build-name is used as CFBundleShortVersionString while build-number is used as CFBundleVersion.
      # Read more about iOS versioning at
      # https://developer.apple.com/library/archive/documentation/General/Reference/InfoPlistKeyReference/Articles/CoreFoundationKeys.html
      # In Windows, build-name is used as the major, minor, and patch parts
      # of the product and file versions while build-number is used as the build suffix.
      version: 1.0.0+1
      
      environment:
        sdk: ^3.13.2
      
      # Dependencies specify other packages that your package needs in order to work.
      # To automatically upgrade your package dependencies to the latest versions
      # consider running `flutter pub upgrade --major-versions`. Alternatively,
      # dependencies can be manually updated by changing the version numbers below to
      # the latest version available on pub.dev. To see which dependencies have newer
      # versions available, run `flutter pub outdated`.
      dependencies:
        flutter:
          sdk: flutter
      
        # The following adds the Cupertino Icons font to your application.
        # Use with the CupertinoIcons class for iOS style icons.
        cupertino_icons: ^1.0.8
        go_router: ^18.0.1
        google_sign_in: ^6.3.0
        provider: ^6.1.5+1
        firebase_core: ^4.15.0
        firebase_auth: ^6.7.0
        fl_chart: ^0.68.0
        # The Scan tab reads a printed prescription with the camera: `camera` shows a
        # live preview and captures a frame, `image_picker` offers a photo from the
        # gallery as an alternative source, and ML Kit's on-device text recognition
        # turns the picture into lines of text. The Latin OCR model is not in the APK;
        # Google Play Services downloads it on first use and it works offline
        # afterwards. Android and iOS only; on web the feature reports itself
        # unavailable rather than failing.
        google_mlkit_text_recognition: ^0.17.1
        camera: ^0.12.1
        image_picker: ^1.2.1
        cloud_firestore: ^6.10.0
        connectivity_plus: ^7.3.1
        shared_preferences: ^2.5.5
        package_info_plus: ^10.2.2
      
      dev_dependencies:
        flutter_test:
          sdk: flutter
      
        # The "flutter_lints" package below contains a set of recommended lints to
        # encourage good coding practices. The lint set provided by the package is
        # activated in the `analysis_options.yaml` file located at the root of your
        # package. See that file for information about deactivating specific lint
        # rules and activating additional ones.
        flutter_lints: ^6.0.0
        flutter_launcher_icons: ^0.14.4
        integration_test:
          sdk: flutter
        excel: ^4.0.6
        # Not used by the app, only by `test/connection_source_test.dart`, which swaps
        # `ConnectivityPlatform.instance` for a fake so the platform channel can be
        # driven without a device. The channel names and the list-of-transports shape
        # both come from here, so the fake follows the real contract rather than a
        # guess at it.
        connectivity_plus_platform_interface: ^2.1.0
        # Not used by the app, only by `test/firestore_medicine_store_test.dart`. An
        # in-memory `FirebaseFirestore`, so the store's confirmation logic and its
        # replay-before-`enableNetwork` ordering can be driven without an emulator.
        # Pinned to a release whose `cloud_firestore` constraint matches pubspec.lock.
        fake_cloud_firestore: ^4.3.0
        # Used only by `test/firestore_medicine_store_test.dart`, to make the fake
        # Firestore refuse one write so the store's refusal-versus-transport-failure
        # split can be tested. A direct dependency because the test imports it.
        mock_exceptions: ^0.8.2
      
      # For information on the generic Dart part of this file, see the
      # following page: https://dart.dev/tools/pub/pubspec
      
      # The following section is specific to Flutter packages.
      flutter:
      
        # The following line ensures that the Material Icons font is
        # included with your application, so that you can use the icons in
        # the material Icons class.
        uses-material-design: true
      
        assets:
          - assets/images/
          # The price list and sales history rebuilt from `Daily Sales Tracker.xlsx` by
          # `tool/import_sales_tracker.dart`. The price list is seeded into Firestore on
          # first run; the sales history is read and totalled in memory, since it
          # describes months that have already happened. The spreadsheet itself is not
          # an asset: the app reads JSON, never a spreadsheet.
          - assets/data/medicines_seed.json
          - assets/data/sales_seed.json
          - assets/data/cleaned_sales_seed.json
      
        fonts:
          - family: PlayfairDisplay
            fonts:
              - asset: assets/fonts/PlayfairDisplay-Variable.ttf
      
        # To add assets to your application, add an assets section, like this:
        # assets:
        #   - images/a_dot_burr.jpeg
        #   - images/a_dot_ham.jpeg
      
        # An image asset can refer to one or more resolution-specific "variants", see

        # For details regarding adding assets from package dependencies, see

        # For details regarding fonts from package dependencies,
        # see https://flutter.dev/to/font-from-package
      
      flutter_launcher_icons:
        android: true
        image_path: "assets/images/baseIcon.png"
